- **team-build**: Build and push the project's release artifact for a target environment (dev | stage | prod), driven entirely by the team config's `build` block. Dev is typically automatic after /team-ship merge. Prod policy is set by config.build.prodPolicy (commonly "refuse" — prod is CI-only). Stack-agnostic. ([fuzemobi/claude-plugins](https://github.com/fuzemobi/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/team-mode/commands/team-build.md))
- **team-build**: Build an agent team architecture from an approved design spec — hard-rejects if the spec is missing, unapproved, or tampered (delegates to harness-factory). If harness-factory is installed, reads its team-build SKILL.md and executes it; otherwise shows an install guide. - **temporal-cloud-setup**: Set up Temporal Cloud and run a sample Workflow on it for the user, doing the work end to end. This is the Cloud setup path, not the local learning path (see temporal-getting-started). Covers Python, TypeScript, Go, Java, .NET, and Ruby SDKs. - **temporal-correctness-audit**: Sweep every date anchor, staleness calculation, cron mirror and row-vs-date window in the repo against the "Dates & scheduling" bug cluster — the single largest recurrence group in the recurring-bug rules, held in bugs-data-layer.md (~35 recorded instances), only 2 of whose 6 signatures are statically enforced ([kanchankapila/bharat-stock-intelligence](https://github.com/kanchankapila/bharat-stock-intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/temporal-correctness-audit.md))
- **temporal-developer**: Develop, debug, and manage Temporal applications across Python, TypeScript, Go, Java, .NET, Ruby, and Rust. Use when the user is building workflows, activities, or workers with a Temporal SDK, debugging issues like non-determinism errors, stuck workflows, or activity retries, using Temporal CLI, Temporal Server, or Temporal Cloud, or working with durable execution concepts like signals, queries, heartbeats, versioning, continue-as-new, child workflows, or saga patterns. Also use when the user mentions "run a Temporal workflow from the CLI", "start a dev server", "run temporal server start-dev… ([vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master](https://github.com/vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master/blob/HEAD/skills/temporal-developer/commands/temporal-developer.md))
- **temporal-logic**: Install (if needed) and run the `temporal-logic` cog on the paired seed ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/temporal-logic/commands/temporal-logic.md) · ★3)
- **temporal-ops**: Administer and diagnose running Temporal Cloud or self-hosted Temporal Server environments via CLI (temporal, tcld) — not SDK code. Operations: namespace CRUD, Cloud capacity/APS, API-key rotation, mTLS cert rotation, workflow health, batch cancel/terminate/reset, export, search attributes, Ops API, billing, audit logs, Terraform, SAML/SCIM, migration. Diagnosis: bottom-up triage of stuck workflows, non-determinism, worker-health, task-queue problems, HA failover, payload size limits, performance bottlenecks, missed schedules. Do NOT trigger for generic TLS/gRPC errors unrelated to Temporal,… ([vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master](https://github.com/vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master/blob/HEAD/skills/temporal-ops/commands/temporal-ops.md))
- **temporal-serverless**: Deploy and operate Temporal Workers on serverless compute (AWS Lambda) driven by the Worker Controller Instance (WCI). ([vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master](https://github.com/vatsalmehta09/Backend-Master/blob/HEAD/skills/temporal-serverless/commands/temporal-serverless.md))
